The getters task b couldn't make const (EndOfPlanTasks.txt, b)
================================================================

Each library's getters were made const where the compiler allows it.  These are the ones it doesn't, by why, each
with the first error (its file and line as they were then).  b doesn't change what a getter returns, so a getter that
hands out a writable pointer or reference into its object stays as it is: they are yours to decide.

  - Hands out a writable pointer into its object: it returns &mcThing, an array of its own, or what a member's
    non-const function returns (most often CChars::Text's char*), C2440 and C2663.
  - Calls something of its object's that isn't const: another function that changes the object, or one that
    hands out a pointer (an array's Get(i), a list's GetHead), C2662.
  - Takes the address of a Ptr<> member, (CPins*)&mpPins: Ptr<>'s operator& (the address of what it points to) isn't
    const, so in a const getter &mpPins is the Ptr's own address, which the cast takes with no error.  Kept back
    without asking the compiler (found when TestMicroprocessorLib crashed).  Ptr<> keeps its operator& as it is: a
    const one was tried before and skipped the copy constructor's memory management (the user, 2026-11-17).
  - Other: anything else the compiler said.

Totals
------

                    Getters                      Virtual getters (declarations)
                    made const   couldn't be     made const   couldn't be
  CommonLibraries         2149          1173            510           304
  HCP65-IDE                236           188             11             9
  HCP65                    145            58             32            16
  LogiCim                  156            65             33            47
  HCP65-Sculpt              17            13              0             0
  WindowInputTester          0             0              0             0
  All                     2703          1497            586           376

The virtual getters are 185 families made const and 132 kept as they were (a family is const whole or not at all).
Of the getters that couldn't be, 696 hand out a writable pointer, 570 call something that isn't const, 27 take a
Ptr<>'s address and 204 are other.


For you to decide
-----------------

What keeps most of the 1497 back is a few things, each the first error of many getters.  Each is a change to what a
getter returns or to a class's members, which b doesn't make.  Once one is decided, the loop (add const, build, give
back what breaks) can be run again over what it frees.

  1. CChars::Text (and CCharsImmutable's) has no const overload: char* Text(void) only.  221 getters' first error is
     calling it, GetName's family among them.  A const char* Text(void) const beside it would free those that only
     read the text; those that return its char* would need to return const char*, and their callers to take it.
  2. Ptr<>'s operator-> isn't const: 169 getters' first error (C2678).  You ruled out a const operator-> and
     operator& on 2026-11-17 (it skipped the copy constructor's memory management), so these stay unless Ptr<> is
     given another way to read through a const Ptr.
  3. Getters that hand out a pointer or a reference into their object: 444 (C2440: GetData, GetArray, GetFiles and
     the like).  Either a const pair (const X* GetX(void) const beside X* GetX(void)) or const returns, which change
     their callers.
  4. The containers' element getters aren't const: CArrayTemplate's Get(i), GetPtr(i) and Tail, CLinkedList's
     GetHead and GetNext, CMapBlock's, 98 first errors.  Const overloads on the containers would free them and,
     through them, chains of getters: CArrayTemplate::Get keeps CArrayBlockSorted::GetHoldingSize back, which keeps
     CArrayBlockSorted::NumElements, then CMapBlock::NumElements, then the NumElements family of CArrayCommonObject,
     CCollection, CIndexObject and CMapObject, which keeps 47 more.
  5. Getters that lock: CMutex::Lock and CInterProcessMutex::Lock, 27 first errors.  The mutex as a mutable member
     would let them be const.
  6. Getters that do work when asked: CSceneFileReader's NumElements, NumChunks and GetInt32 read the file (48 first
     errors); CNumber::GetLastNonZeroDigit (the scan didn't take it for a getter) keeps IsEven, IsOdd, IsInteger and
     GetDecimals back.  Either they're not getters, or what they work out is a mutable cache.
  7. A virtual family stays as it is when any member can't be const: 132 families.  Overrides that macros write
     (CONSTRUCTABLE's ClassSize, CPPSyntax's GetKind) are in a family with the classes the macro is used in.


BaseLib: 409 made const, 237 couldn't be
----------------------------------------
